Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- ogx-k8s-operator is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 2 CRD(s)\: LlamaStackDistribution, OGXServer.
- deploying 3 workload(s) (deployment, ogx-k8s-operator-controller-manager, operator) with 2 container(s).
- watching 19 resource type(s).
- exposing 2 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443.
- with 1 webhook(s) (1 validating).
- protected by 1 network policy/policies.
- with 2 PodDisruptionBudget(s).
- with 1 HorizontalPodAutoscaler(s).
- runtime dependencies\: PostgreSQL.
- defining 26 API spec type(s) across 58 struct(s) with 210 total fields.
- with 180 operator config constants.
- reporting 20 status condition types.
- with 1 ingress route(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- kustomize overlays\: config/certmanager, config/crd, config/default, config/manager, config/manifests, config/overlays/cert-manager, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/openshift, config/overlays/rhoai, config/prometheus, config/rbac, config/scorecard, config/webhook.
- mounts 1 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: config.
